{"parentUuid":"c1e6d5b9-a1ff-4939-a4b6-ad85b0af406c","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"8377c9b6-2144-4df6-b2f1-060350fd81e8","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SFPiHwTyQy7bJQwvVRDUL4","type":"tool_result","content":"## Implementation Decisions\n\n### Modules\n\n- **Accounts:** Users, argon2 hashing, login and logout, the signed session cookie, and the rule that a Deactivated User is refused both at login and on every request.\n- **Permissions:** the role check shared by all console routes. Editor: Posts and the dashboard. Admin: everything. It's enforced server-side, and a refusal returns 403.\n- **CSRF:** a per-session token, a hidden field in every state-changing form, and a check on every non-GET console request.\n- **Content:** Posts and Pages, their Draft/Published lifecycle, Slug generation, clash handling and locking, Event-date validation, and the queries for Current menu, Upcoming and the feeds. The console and the Export share it, so both use the same definitions.\n- **Markdown rendering:** one function, used by both Preview and Export, that turns Markdown into sanitized HTML (markdown-it, then nh3). It allows `img` only with an `http(s)` source.\n- **Console routes and templates:** login, dashboard, Post list and editor, Page list and editor, Users, and Confirmation screens. The console is in English.\n- **Export:** `render_site` reads the database and writes the Spanish public site. The `cms publish` and `cms deploy` commands together make up an Export.\n- **Time:** helpers for UTC storage, America/Lima display (standard-library `zoneinfo`) and Spanish date formatting from a hard-coded month list. No new dependency.\n\n### Schema (SQLite; no ORM, following ADR-002's stack)\n\n- **users:** id, name, email (unique, case-insensitive), password_hash, role (`admin` | `editor`), active, created_at.\n- **posts:** id, kind (`news` | `event` | `menu`, fixed at creation), title, slug, body_md, status (`draft` | `published`), author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at, and for Events only: event_start_date, event_start_time (nullable), event_end_date (nullable). The slug is unique within a kind.\n- **pages:** id, title, slug (unique), body_md, status, nav_order, author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at.\n- **exports:** the time each Export ran, plus a count of Published items deleted since then, used by the not-yet-Live indicator.\n- All timestamps are stored in UTC.\n\n### Rules\n\n- **Slug:** generated from the title, lower-case ASCII with accents stripped and hyphens between words. An auto-generated clash gets a numeric suffix; a slug typed by hand that clashes is a form error. The slug can be edited only while `first_published_at` is empty, and once set, that field is never cleared, even by Unpublishing.\n- **Reserved Page Slugs:** `index`, `noticias`, `eventos`, `menus`, `style`.\n- **Event date:** a start date is required. The end date must be on or after the start date. Event fields are rejected on News and Menu Posts.\n- **Upcoming:** `coalesce(event_end_date, event_start_date) >= export_date`, where the export date is a date in America/Lima. The Home page shows the next 3 Upcoming Events.\n- **Current menu:** the Published Menu with the latest `first_published_at`. Every other Published Menu goes to the Menu archive, newest first.\n- **Home page:** the Current menu, the next 3 Upcoming Events and the latest 5 News, all on one page.\n- **Confirmation (no JavaScript):** submitting a change that Members will see (publish, unpublish, delete, or saving Published content) returns a Confirmation page. That page shows a summary and, where relevant, the rendered preview, and carries the pending values in hidden fields with the CSRF token. Only a second POST that includes the confirm flag applies the change. Cancel returns to the editor with the values intact. Saving a Draft applies immediately.\n- **Self-protection:** a request that would deactivate the Admin making it, or change that Admin's own role, is refused with an explanation.\n- **Not-yet-Live indicator:** Posts and Pages whose `updated_at` is after the last Export, plus Published items deleted since then. If there has never been an Export, every Published item counts.\n- **First Admin:** the seed script creates the first Admin and Editor, taking their passwords from `CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD` and `CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD`. No separate bootstrap command.\n\n### Public site layout (all links relative)\n\n- `index.html`: the Home page.\n- `noticias/index.html` and `noticias/<slug>.html`: News.\n- `eventos/index.html` and `eventos/<slug>.html`: Events (Upcoming, then past).\n- `menus/index.html` and `menus/<slug>.html`: the Menu archive and each Menu.\n- `<slug>.html`: Pages.\n- `style.css`.\n- Navigation order: Inicio, Noticias, Eventos, Menús anteriores, then Pages by nav_order (ties broken by title).\n- The site title comes from `CMS_SITE_TITLE`, set to the club's name in `.env.example`.\n\n### Interfaces that change\n\n- `create_app()` accepts a database path (default from settings), so tests can give each app its own database.\n- `render_site(out, export_date=None)` reads Published content from the configured database. `export_date` defaults to today in America/Lima and records the Export.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- **A good test** exercises behaviour through a public seam and asserts only what a User or Member could observe: status codes, redirects, rendered console HTML, and the files and HTML written to `site/`. Tests never query tables or call internal helpers. That lets the storage and module layout change without rewriting tests.\n- **Seam 1, the console over HTTP:** `create_app()` driven by the existing `client` and `client_as(\"admin\" | \"editor\")` fixtures. This covers login and deactivation, Permissions (Editor gets 403 on Pages and Users), CSRF rejection, the Post and Page lifecycle, Slug rules, Event-date validation, Confirmation (the first POST changes nothing; the confirmed POST applies), User management and self-protection, the dashboard counts and filters.\n- **Seam 2, Export:** `render_site(tmp_path, export_date=...)`, asserting on the output files. This covers Drafts never being written, the Current menu versus the Menu archive, the Upcoming boundary (the weekend tournament on its last day, and the day after), Navigation order, Spanish dates in Lima time, no Author names, sanitized Markdown (a `<script>` is stripped and an external image survives), and relative paths only.\n- **Fixture change:** each test gets a fresh temporary database seeded with the `DEMO_USERS` admin and editor from the conftest file, so `client_as` works and tests never share state.\n- **Prior art:** `tests/test_t00.py`, which checks the admin page answers over HTTP, that `render_site` writes files, and that paths are relative. `tests/conftest.py` provides the `client_as` pattern. The T00 tests must keep passing.\n- No tests below these two seams.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- Media uploads. Images are only Markdown links to files hosted elsewhere.\n- Review or approval workflows, and any status beyond Draft and Published.\n- Scheduled publishing.\n- The office running Exports; there's no Export button in the console.\n- JavaScript on the public site, including client-side Upcoming filtering.\n- Member logins, comments and search.\n- Self-service password change and password reset.\n- Deleting Users.\n- Changing a Post's kind after creation.\n- Multi-site, drag-and-drop page builders and WYSIWYG editors (excluded by the assignment).\n- A Spanish admin console.\n- Deploying the admin console anywhere public (ADR-001).\n\n## Further Notes\n\n- The CLI keeps the assignment's command names. In the glossary, running `cms publish` and then `cms deploy` is one Export. The word \"publish\" in the console only ever means the Draft → Published status change.\n- A stale Upcoming list between Exports is accepted: the fix is to Export often, not to add JavaScript.\n- Confirmation is the club's main defence against a wrong price. It applies to Published content edits as well as status changes, because a Live Menu is most often corrected by editing it.\n- Every console route must require login. The permission check applies to routes, not to navigation links.\n\n---\n.agents/skills/ask-matt/PHASE-BOUNDARIES.md\n.agents/skills/ask-matt/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/ask-matt/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/claude-handoff/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/claude-handoff/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/code-review/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/code-review/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/codebase-design/DEEPENING.md\n=== app/__init__.py\n\"\"\"IPHS 400 MP2 — your CMS package.\n\nT00 ships a skeleton only: one admin page, one public page, and a publish step.\nEverything else (login, roles, posts, pages, users) is yours to build, one\nticket at a time.\n\"\"\"\n=== app/cli.py\n\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        out = render_site()\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\n             str(settings.SITE)],\n        )\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\n        return result.returncode\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n=== app/main.py\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nT00 (already done): the admin console answers at /admin and the public site\nanswers at /. That is the whole skeleton — it exists so you can prove the stack\nruns before you build anything on it.\n\nAdd your routes in their own modules (app/routes/posts.py and so on) and include\nthem here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import settings\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n\n\ndef create_app() -> FastAPI:\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\")\n\n    @app.get(\"/admin\")\n    def admin_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"admin/hello.html\", {\"title\": \"Admin\"}\n        )\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # Your ticket work plugs in here, e.g.\n    #   from app.routes import posts\n    #   app.include_router(posts.router)\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n=== app/publish.py\n\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n            home_path=\"index.html\",\n        )\n    )\n    return out\n=== app/settings.py\n\"\"\"Configuration, read from the environment (never hard-code secrets).\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nTEMPLATES = ROOT / \"templates\"\nSITE = ROOT / \"site\"\n\nSECRET_KEY = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SECRET_KEY\", \"dev-only-not-for-production\")\nDATABASE_PATH = Path(os.environ.get(\"CMS_DATABASE\", ROOT / \"cms.db\"))\nSITE_TITLE = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SITE_TITLE\", \"My CMS\")\n# Set this to your Pages URL once you deploy, e.g.\n# https://yourname.github.io/iphs400-mp2-cms/\nBASE_PATH = os.environ.get(\"CMS_BASE_PATH\", \"\")\n=== scripts/seed_demo.py\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create demo data so a grader (and you) can use the CMS immediately.\n\n    uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\n\nT00 has nothing to seed. As you build content types, extend this so it creates:\n  - one admin and one editor (passwords read from .env, never hard-coded)\n  - a few posts and pages, at least one draft and one published\n\nThe rubric expects this to run clean on a fresh clone with .env.example values\n(item E4), because the database itself is never committed.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    admin_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\")\n    editor_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\")\n    if not admin_pw or not editor_pw:\n        print(\"Set CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD and CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD in .env \"\n              \"(copy .env.example).\")\n        return 1\n\n    # TODO (your tickets): create the users, then the demo content.\n    print(\"Nothing to seed yet: no content types exist. \"\n          \"Extend scripts/seed_demo.py as you build T01+.\")\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n=== tests/conftest.py\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client() -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(create_app())\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as():\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(create_app())\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"]},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        if response.status_code == 404:\n            pytest.skip(\"No /login route yet — build the login ticket first.\")\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n=== tests/test_t00.py\n\"\"\"T00: the walking skeleton. These pass in the fresh template.\n\nDo not delete them. If a change breaks them, the change broke the app's front\ndoor.\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef test_admin_console_answers(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/admin\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    assert \"hello admin\" in response.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_public_home_answers(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_publish_writes_a_site(tmp_path):\n    from app.publish import render_site\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    assert (out / \"index.html\").exists() and (out / \"style.css\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_published_html_uses_relative_paths(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Root-absolute paths break on GitHub Pages project URLs (rubric F2).\"\"\"\n    from app.publish import render_site\n\n    html = (render_site(tmp_path / \"site\") / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert 'href=\"/' not in html and 'src=\"/' not in html\n=== templates/base.html\n<!doctype html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n  <title>{% block title %}{{ title }}{% endblock %}</title>\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"{{ css_path | default('style.css') }}\">\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header><a href=\"{{ home_path | default('index.html') }}\">{{ title }}</a></header>\n  <main>{% block content %}{% endblock %}</main>\n  <footer><small>IPHS 400 Mini-Project #2</small></footer>\n</body>\n</html>\n=== templates/admin/hello.html\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>T00: hello admin</h1>\n  <p>The stack works. Now build ticket T01.</p>\n{% endblock %}\n=== .env.example\n# Copy to .env (which is gitignored) and change the values.\nCMS_SECRET_KEY=change-me-to-a-long-random-string\nCMS_SITE_TITLE=Knox County Historical Society\nCMS_DATABASE=cms.db\n# Demo accounts created by scripts/seed_demo.py\nCMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD=change-me-admin\nCMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD=change-me-editor\n# Set after you deploy, e.g. https://yourname.github.io/iphs400-mp2-cms/\nCMS_BASE_PATH=\n=== .gitignore\n.env\n*.db\nsite/\n__pycache__/\n.pytest_cache/\n.venv/\n.claude/state/\n.DS_Store\n# ADR-002: FastAPI + Jinja + SQLite, with no frontend framework\n\n**Status:** accepted (set by the assignment)\n**Date:** 2026-09-22\n\n## Context\n\nThe project has to be readable by someone who has never built a web application,\ngradable from a clean clone, and finishable in two weeks on a $20 plan. Options\nconsidered: Django (batteries included, but a large amount of generated code to\nread), Flask (similar to FastAPI here), a JavaScript stack (a second language and\na build step), and FastAPI with server-rendered templates.\n\n## Decision\n\nFastAPI with Jinja2 templates, SQLite for storage, argon2 for password hashing,\nand an HTML sanitizer for user Markdown. No frontend framework, no build step.\n\n## Consequences\n\n- Every page is server-rendered, so \"view source\" shows what the code produced,\n  which makes debugging visible.\n- SQLite is a single file, so a grader can seed and inspect it easily. It is\n  gitignored; `scripts/seed_demo.py` rebuilds it.\n- No build step means the file you preview is the file that ships.\n- Interactive flourishes (live preview without reload, drag-and-drop) cost more\n  here than in a JavaScript stack. That is an accepted trade.","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"d68279a6-9ce7-46df-b46a-9a21a65f96b9","timestamp":"2026-09-29T19:45:49.035Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"## Implementation Decisions\n\n### Modules\n\n- **Accounts:** Users, argon2 hashing, login and logout, the signed session cookie, and the rule that a Deactivated User is refused both at login and on every request.\n- **Permissions:** the role check shared by all console routes. Editor: Posts and the dashboard. Admin: everything. It's enforced server-side, and a refusal returns 403.\n- **CSRF:** a per-session token, a hidden field in every state-changing form, and a check on every non-GET console request.\n- **Content:** Posts and Pages, their Draft/Published lifecycle, Slug generation, clash handling and locking, Event-date validation, and the queries for Current menu, Upcoming and the feeds. The console and the Export share it, so both use the same definitions.\n- **Markdown rendering:** one function, used by both Preview and Export, that turns Markdown into sanitized HTML (markdown-it, then nh3). It allows `img` only with an `http(s)` source.\n- **Console routes and templates:** login, dashboard, Post list and editor, Page list and editor, Users, and Confirmation screens. The console is in English.\n- **Export:** `render_site` reads the database and writes the Spanish public site. The `cms publish` and `cms deploy` commands together make up an Export.\n- **Time:** helpers for UTC storage, America/Lima display (standard-library `zoneinfo`) and Spanish date formatting from a hard-coded month list. No new dependency.\n\n### Schema (SQLite; no ORM, following ADR-002's stack)\n\n- **users:** id, name, email (unique, case-insensitive), password_hash, role (`admin` | `editor`), active, created_at.\n- **posts:** id, kind (`news` | `event` | `menu`, fixed at creation), title, slug, body_md, status (`draft` | `published`), author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at, and for Events only: event_start_date, event_start_time (nullable), event_end_date (nullable). The slug is unique within a kind.\n- **pages:** id, title, slug (unique), body_md, status, nav_order, author_id, created_at, updated_at, first_published_at.\n- **exports:** the time each Export ran, plus a count of Published items deleted since then, used by the not-yet-Live indicator.\n- All timestamps are stored in UTC.\n\n### Rules\n\n- **Slug:** generated from the title, lower-case ASCII with accents stripped and hyphens between words. An auto-generated clash gets a numeric suffix; a slug typed by hand that clashes is a form error. The slug can be edited only while `first_published_at` is empty, and once set, that field is never cleared, even by Unpublishing.\n- **Reserved Page Slugs:** `index`, `noticias`, `eventos`, `menus`, `style`.\n- **Event date:** a start date is required. The end date must be on or after the start date. Event fields are rejected on News and Menu Posts.\n- **Upcoming:** `coalesce(event_end_date, event_start_date) >= export_date`, where the export date is a date in America/Lima. The Home page shows the next 3 Upcoming Events.\n- **Current menu:** the Published Menu with the latest `first_published_at`. Every other Published Menu goes to the Menu archive, newest first.\n- **Home page:** the Current menu, the next 3 Upcoming Events and the latest 5 News, all on one page.\n- **Confirmation (no JavaScript):** submitting a change that Members will see (publish, unpublish, delete, or saving Published content) returns a Confirmation page. That page shows a summary and, where relevant, the rendered preview, and carries the pending values in hidden fields with the CSRF token. Only a second POST that includes the confirm flag applies the change. Cancel returns to the editor with the values intact. Saving a Draft applies immediately.\n- **Self-protection:** a request that would deactivate the Admin making it, or change that Admin's own role, is refused with an explanation.\n- **Not-yet-Live indicator:** Posts and Pages whose `updated_at` is after the last Export, plus Published items deleted since then. If there has never been an Export, every Published item counts.\n- **First Admin:** the seed script creates the first Admin and Editor, taking their passwords from `CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD` and `CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD`. No separate bootstrap command.\n\n### Public site layout (all links relative)\n\n- `index.html`: the Home page.\n- `noticias/index.html` and `noticias/<slug>.html`: News.\n- `eventos/index.html` and `eventos/<slug>.html`: Events (Upcoming, then past).\n- `menus/index.html` and `menus/<slug>.html`: the Menu archive and each Menu.\n- `<slug>.html`: Pages.\n- `style.css`.\n- Navigation order: Inicio, Noticias, Eventos, Menús anteriores, then Pages by nav_order (ties broken by title).\n- The site title comes from `CMS_SITE_TITLE`, set to the club's name in `.env.example`.\n\n### Interfaces that change\n\n- `create_app()` accepts a database path (default from settings), so tests can give each app its own database.\n- `render_site(out, export_date=None)` reads Published content from the configured database. `export_date` defaults to today in America/Lima and records the Export.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- **A good test** exercises behaviour through a public seam and asserts only what a User or Member could observe: status codes, redirects, rendered console HTML, and the files and HTML written to `site/`. Tests never query tables or call internal helpers. That lets the storage and module layout change without rewriting tests.\n- **Seam 1, the console over HTTP:** `create_app()` driven by the existing `client` and `client_as(\"admin\" | \"editor\")` fixtures. This covers login and deactivation, Permissions (Editor gets 403 on Pages and Users), CSRF rejection, the Post and Page lifecycle, Slug rules, Event-date validation, Confirmation (the first POST changes nothing; the confirmed POST applies), User management and self-protection, the dashboard counts and filters.\n- **Seam 2, Export:** `render_site(tmp_path, export_date=...)`, asserting on the output files. This covers Drafts never being written, the Current menu versus the Menu archive, the Upcoming boundary (the weekend tournament on its last day, and the day after), Navigation order, Spanish dates in Lima time, no Author names, sanitized Markdown (a `<script>` is stripped and an external image survives), and relative paths only.\n- **Fixture change:** each test gets a fresh temporary database seeded with the `DEMO_USERS` admin and editor from the conftest file, so `client_as` works and tests never share state.\n- **Prior art:** `tests/test_t00.py`, which checks the admin page answers over HTTP, that `render_site` writes files, and that paths are relative. `tests/conftest.py` provides the `client_as` pattern. The T00 tests must keep passing.\n- No tests below these two seams.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- Media uploads. Images are only Markdown links to files hosted elsewhere.\n- Review or approval workflows, and any status beyond Draft and Published.\n- Scheduled publishing.\n- The office running Exports; there's no Export button in the console.\n- JavaScript on the public site, including client-side Upcoming filtering.\n- Member logins, comments and search.\n- Self-service password change and password reset.\n- Deleting Users.\n- Changing a Post's kind after creation.\n- Multi-site, drag-and-drop page builders and WYSIWYG editors (excluded by the assignment).\n- A Spanish admin console.\n- Deploying the admin console anywhere public (ADR-001).\n\n## Further Notes\n\n- The CLI keeps the assignment's command names. In the glossary, running `cms publish` and then `cms deploy` is one Export. The word \"publish\" in the console only ever means the Draft → Published status change.\n- A stale Upcoming list between Exports is accepted: the fix is to Export often, not to add JavaScript.\n- Confirmation is the club's main defence against a wrong price. It applies to Published content edits as well as status changes, because a Live Menu is most often corrected by editing it.\n- Every console route must require login. The permission check applies to routes, not to navigation links.\n\n---\n.agents/skills/ask-matt/PHASE-BOUNDARIES.md\n.agents/skills/ask-matt/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/ask-matt/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/claude-handoff/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/claude-handoff/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/code-review/SKILL.md\n.agents/skills/code-review/agents/openai.yaml\n.agents/skills/codebase-design/DEEPENING.md\n=== app/__init__.py\n\"\"\"IPHS 400 MP2 — your CMS package.\n\nT00 ships a skeleton only: one admin page, one public page, and a publish step.\nEverything else (login, roles, posts, pages, users) is yours to build, one\nticket at a time.\n\"\"\"\n=== app/cli.py\n\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        out = render_site()\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\n             str(settings.SITE)],\n        )\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\n        return result.returncode\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n=== app/main.py\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nT00 (already done): the admin console answers at /admin and the public site\nanswers at /. That is the whole skeleton — it exists so you can prove the stack\nruns before you build anything on it.\n\nAdd your routes in their own modules (app/routes/posts.py and so on) and include\nthem here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import settings\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n\n\ndef create_app() -> FastAPI:\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\")\n\n    @app.get(\"/admin\")\n    def admin_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"admin/hello.html\", {\"title\": \"Admin\"}\n        )\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # Your ticket work plugs in here, e.g.\n    #   from app.routes import posts\n    #   app.include_router(posts.router)\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n=== app/publish.py\n\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n            home_path=\"index.html\",\n        )\n    )\n    return out\n=== app/settings.py\n\"\"\"Configuration, read from the environment (never hard-code secrets).\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nTEMPLATES = ROOT / \"templates\"\nSITE = ROOT / \"site\"\n\nSECRET_KEY = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SECRET_KEY\", \"dev-only-not-for-production\")\nDATABASE_PATH = Path(os.environ.get(\"CMS_DATABASE\", ROOT / \"cms.db\"))\nSITE_TITLE = os.environ.get(\"CMS_SITE_TITLE\", \"My CMS\")\n# Set this to your Pages URL once you deploy, e.g.\n# https://yourname.github.io/iphs400-mp2-cms/\nBASE_PATH = os.environ.get(\"CMS_BASE_PATH\", \"\")\n=== scripts/seed_demo.py\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Create demo data so a grader (and you) can use the CMS immediately.\n\n    uv run python scripts/seed_demo.py\n\nT00 has nothing to seed. As you build content types, extend this so it creates:\n  - one admin and one editor (passwords read from .env, never hard-coded)\n  - a few posts and pages, at least one draft and one published\n\nThe rubric expects this to run clean on a fresh clone with .env.example values\n(item E4), because the database itself is never committed.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    admin_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\")\n    editor_pw = os.environ.get(\"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\")\n    if not admin_pw or not editor_pw:\n        print(\"Set CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD and CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD in .env \"\n              \"(copy .env.example).\")\n        return 1\n\n    # TODO (your tickets): create the users, then the demo content.\n    print(\"Nothing to seed yet: no content types exist. \"\n          \"Extend scripts/seed_demo.py as you build T01+.\")\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n=== tests/conftest.py\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client() -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(create_app())\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as():\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(create_app())\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"]},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        if response.status_code == 404:\n            pytest.skip(\"No /login route yet — build the login ticket first.\")\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n=== tests/test_t00.py\n\"\"\"T00: the walking skeleton. These pass in the fresh template.\n\nDo not delete them. If a change breaks them, the change broke the app's front\ndoor.\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef test_admin_console_answers(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/admin\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    assert \"hello admin\" in response.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_public_home_answers(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_publish_writes_a_site(tmp_path):\n    from app.publish import render_site\n\n    out = render_site(tmp_path / \"site\")\n    assert (out / \"index.html\").exists() and (out / \"style.css\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_published_html_uses_relative_paths(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Root-absolute paths break on GitHub Pages project URLs (rubric F2).\"\"\"\n    from app.publish import render_site\n\n    html = (render_site(tmp_path / \"site\") / \"index.html\").read_text()\n    assert 'href=\"/' not in html and 'src=\"/' not in html\n=== templates/base.html\n<!doctype html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n  <title>{% block title %}{{ title }}{% endblock %}</title>\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"{{ css_path | default('style.css') }}\">\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header><a href=\"{{ home_path | default('index.html') }}\">{{ title }}</a></header>\n  <main>{% block content %}{% endblock %}</main>\n  <footer><small>IPHS 400 Mini-Project #2</small></footer>\n</body>\n</html>\n=== templates/admin/hello.html\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>T00: hello admin</h1>\n  <p>The stack works. Now build ticket T01.</p>\n{% endblock %}\n=== .env.example\n# Copy to .env (which is gitignored) and change the values.\nCMS_SECRET_KEY=change-me-to-a-long-random-string\nCMS_SITE_TITLE=Knox County Historical Society\nCMS_DATABASE=cms.db\n# Demo accounts created by scripts/seed_demo.py\nCMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD=change-me-admin\nCMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD=change-me-editor\n# Set after you deploy, e.g. https://yourname.github.io/iphs400-mp2-cms/\nCMS_BASE_PATH=\n=== .gitignore\n.env\n*.db\nsite/\n__pycache__/\n.pytest_cache/\n.venv/\n.claude/state/\n.DS_Store\n# ADR-002: FastAPI + Jinja + SQLite, with no frontend framework\n\n**Status:** accepted (set by the assignment)\n**Date:** 2026-09-22\n\n## Context\n\nThe project has to be readable by someone who has never built a web application,\ngradable from a clean clone, and finishable in two weeks on a $20 plan. Options\nconsidered: Django (batteries included, but a large amount of generated code to\nread), Flask (similar to FastAPI here), a JavaScript stack (a second language and\na build step), and FastAPI with server-rendered templates.\n\n## Decision\n\nFastAPI with Jinja2 templates, SQLite for storage, argon2 for password hashing,\nand an HTML sanitizer for user Markdown. No frontend framework, no build step.\n\n## Consequences\n\n- Every page is server-rendered, so \"view source\" shows what the code produced,\n  which makes debugging visible.\n- SQLite is a single file, so a grader can seed and inspect it easily. It is\n  gitignored; `scripts/seed_demo.py` rebuilds it.\n- No build step means the file you preview is the file that ships.\n- Interactive flourishes (live preview without reload, drag-and-drop) cost more\n  here than in a JavaScript stack. That is an accepted trade.","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"serverClassifierContext":{"request":"0fbcc5e1-f36e-4c74-81fd-ee4f910af533","context":{"git_state":{"cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","root":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","branch":"main","default_branch":"main","status":{"clean":false,"counts":{"staged":0,"modified":1,"untracked":null,"untracked_normal":2},"porcelain":null,"truncated":false},"visibility":{"origin":{"host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"},"push_remote":"origin","remotes":[{"name":"origin","host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}],"visibility_cache":[{"host":"github.com","remote":"gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","visibility":"public"}]}},"live_cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","platform":"wsl"}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c1e6d5b9-a1ff-4939-a4b6-ad85b0af406c","session_id":"b153f3e7-a2f8-4fff-aa8c-7de5013ddc1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"b9c18301-f0fd-4769-b96a-b7176ffb00ff","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\nCSRF_FIELD = re.compile(r'name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"([^\"]+)\"')\n\n\ndef csrf_from(response) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Pull the CSRF token out of a rendered form.\"\"\"\n    match = CSRF_FIELD.search(response.text)\n    assert match, \"no csrf_token field in the page\"\n    return match.group(1)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef db_path(tmp_path):\n    path = tmp_path / \"test.db\"\n    seed_users(path, admin_password=DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"password\"],\n               editor_password=DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"][\"password\"])\n    return path\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client(db_path) -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(create_app(db_path))\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as(db_path):\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a signed-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(create_app(db_path))\n        token = csrf_from(c.get(\"/login\"))\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"],\n                                          \"csrf_token\": token},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        assert response.status_code in (302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\nEOF\npython3 - <<'EOF'\nimport re\np='tests/test_t00.py'\ns=open(p).read()\ns=s.replace('''def test_admin_console_answers(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/admin\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    assert \"hello <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"T01: login, logout, the two roles, and CSRF — all through the console over HTTP.\"\"\"\nimport sqlite3\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import accounts\nfrom app.main import create_app\nfrom tests.conftest import DEMO_USERS, csrf_from\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nADMIN = DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"]\n\n\ndef sign_in(client, email, password):\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(\"/login\"))\n    return client.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": email, \"password\": password,\n                                       \"csrf_token\": token},\n                       follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef test_login_redirects_into_the_console(client):\n    response = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"], ADMIN[\"password\"])\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert client.get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_email_is_case_insensitive(client):\n    response = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"].upper(), ADMIN[\"password\"])\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_wrong_email_and_wrong_password_look_identical(client):\n    unknown = sign_in(client, \"nobody@example.test\", \"whatever\")\n    wrong_pw = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"], \"not-the-password\")\n    assert unknown.status_code == wrong_pw.status_code == 401\n    assert unknown.text == wrong_pw.text  # includes the same CSRF token: same session\n    assert \"wrong email or password\" in unknown.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_password_never_appears_in_response_or_logs(client, caplog):\n    caplog.set_level(\"DEBUG\")\n    bad = \"hunter2-very-secret\"\n    response = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"], bad)\n    assert bad not in response.text\n    assert bad not in caplog.text\n    ok = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"], ADMIN[\"password\"])\n    assert ADMIN[\"password\"] not in ok.text\n    assert ADMIN[\"password\"] not in caplog.text\n\n\ndef test_passwords_are_stored_as_argon2_hashes(db_path):\n    # The one place a test reads storage: the criterion is about storage itself.\n    rows = sqlite3.connect(db_path).execute(\n        \"SELECT password_hash FROM users\").fetchall()\n    assert rows\n    for (stored,) in rows:\n        assert stored.startswith(\"$argon2\")\n        assert \"test-admin-pw\" not in stored and \"test-editor-pw\" not in stored\n\n\ndef test_logout_ends_the_session(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"admin\")\n    assert c.get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200\n    token = csrf_from(c.get(\"/admin\"))\n    assert c.post(\"/logout\", data={\"csrf_token\": token},\n                  follow_redirects=False).status_code == 303\n    later = c.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False)\n    assert later.status_code == 303 and later.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\"\n\n\ndef test_logout_invalidates_a_copied_session_cookie(client_as, db_path):\n    c = client_as(\"admin\")\n    stolen = dict(c.cookies)\n    c.post(\"/logout\", data={\"csrf_token\": csrf_from(c.get(\"/admin\"))})\n    replay = TestClient(create_app(db_path), cookies=stolen)\n    assert replay.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code == 303\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"path\", [\"/admin\", \"/admin/pages\", \"/admin/users\",\n                                  \"/admin/anything-else\"])\ndef test_signed_out_visitor_is_sent_to_login(client, path):\n    response = client.get(path, follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\"\n\n\ndef test_deactivated_user_cannot_sign_in(client, db_path):\n    accounts.set_active(db_path, DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"][\"email\"], False)\n    response = sign_in(client, DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"][\"email\"],\n                       DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"][\"password\"])\n    assert response.status_code == 401\n    assert client.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_user_deactivated_while_signed_in_is_refused_next_request(client_as, db_path):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert c.get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200\n    accounts.set_active(db_path, DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"][\"email\"], False)\n    response = c.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code == 303 and response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\"\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"path\", [\"/admin/pages\", \"/admin/users\"])\ndef test_editor_is_refused_from_admin_areas_and_admin_is_not(client_as, path):\n    assert client_as(\"editor\").get(path).status_code == 403\n    assert client_as(\"admin\").get(path).status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_editor_can_reach_the_dashboard(client_as):\n    assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_editor_does_not_see_admin_navigation(client_as):\n    html = client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert \"/admin/users\" not in html and \"/admin/pages\" not in html\n\n\ndef test_login_without_a_valid_csrf_token_is_rejected(client):\n    creds = {\"email\": ADMIN[\"email\"], \"password\": ADMIN[\"password\"]}\n    assert client.post(\"/login\", data=creds).status_code == 403\n    client.get(\"/login\")\n    assert client.post(\"/login\", data={**creds, \"csrf_token\": \"forged\"}\n                       ).status_code == 403\n    assert client.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_logout_without_a_valid_csrf_token_is_rejected(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"admin\")\n    assert c.post(\"/logout\").status_code == 403\n    assert c.post(\"/logout\", data={\"csrf_token\": \"forged\"}).status_code == 403\n    assert c.get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200  # still signed in\n\n\ndef test_a_token_from_another_session_is_rejected(client_as, db_path):\n    other = TestClient(create_app(db_path))\n    foreign = csrf_from(other.get(\"/login\"))\n    c = client_as(\"admin\")\n    assert c.post(\"/logout\", data={\"csrf_token\": foreign}).status_code == 403\n\n\ndef test_each_app_gets_its_own_database(tmp_path):\n    a, b = tmp_path / \"a.db\", tmp_path / \"b.db\"\n    accounts.seed_users(a, admin_password=\"pw-a\", editor_password=\"pw-e\")\n    ca, cb = TestClient(create_app(a)), TestClient(create_app(b))\n    assert sign_in(ca, ADMIN[\"email\"], \"pw-a\").status_code == 303\n    assert sign_in(cb, ADMIN[\"email\"], \"pw-a\").status_code == <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"T01: login, logout, the two roles, and CSRF — all through the console over HTTP.\"\"\"\nimport sqlite3\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app import accounts\nfrom app.main import create_app\nfrom tests.conftest import DEMO_USERS, csrf_from\n\nROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]\nADMIN = DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"]\n\n\ndef sign_in(client, email, password):\n    token = csrf_from(client.get(\"/login\"))\n    return client.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": email, \"password\": password,\n                                       \"csrf_token\": token},\n                       follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef test_login_redirects_into_the_console(client):\n    response = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"], ADMIN[\"password\"])\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert client.get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_email_is_case_insensitive(client):\n    response = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"].upper(), ADMIN[\"password\"])\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_wrong_email_and_wrong_password_look_identical(client):\n    unknown = sign_in(client, \"nobody@example.test\", \"whatever\")\n    wrong_pw = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"], \"not-the-password\")\n    assert unknown.status_code == wrong_pw.status_code == 401\n    assert unknown.text == wrong_pw.text  # includes the same CSRF token: same session\n    assert \"wrong email or password\" in unknown.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_password_never_appears_in_response_or_logs(client, caplog):\n    caplog.set_level(\"DEBUG\")\n    bad = \"hunter2-very-secret\"\n    response = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"], bad)\n    assert bad not in response.text\n    assert bad not in caplog.text\n    ok = sign_in(client, ADMIN[\"email\"], ADMIN[\"password\"])\n    assert ADMIN[\"password\"] not in ok.text\n    assert ADMIN[\"password\"] not in caplog.text\n\n\ndef test_passwords_are_stored_as_argon2_hashes(db_path):\n    # The one place a test reads storage: the criterion is about storage itself.\n    rows = sqlite3.connect(db_path).execute(\n        \"SELECT password_hash FROM users\").fetchall()\n    assert rows\n    for (stored,) in rows:\n        assert stored.startswith(\"$argon2\")\n        assert \"test-admin-pw\" not in stored and \"test-editor-pw\" not in stored\n\n\ndef test_logout_ends_the_session(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"admin\")\n    assert c.get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200\n    token = csrf_from(c.get(\"/admin\"))\n    assert c.post(\"/logout\", data={\"csrf_token\": token},\n                  follow_redirects=False).status_code == 303\n    later = c.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False)\n    assert later.status_code == 303 and later.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\"\n\n\ndef test_logout_invalidates_a_copied_session_cookie(client_as, db_path):\n    c = client_as(\"admin\")\n    stolen = dict(c.cookies)\n    c.post(\"/logout\", data={\"csrf_token\": csrf_from(c.get(\"/admin\"))})\n    replay = TestClient(create_app(db_path), cookies=stolen)\n    assert replay.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code == 303\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"path\", [\"/admin\", \"/admin/pages\", \"/admin/users\",\n                                  \"/admin/anything-else\"])\ndef test_signed_out_visitor_is_sent_to_login(client, path):\n    response = client.get(path, follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\"\n\n\ndef test_deactivated_user_cannot_sign_in(client, db_path):\n    accounts.set_active(db_path, DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"][\"email\"], False)\n    response = sign_in(client, DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"][\"email\"],\n                       DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"][\"password\"])\n    assert response.status_code == 401\n    assert client.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_user_deactivated_while_signed_in_is_refused_next_request(client_as, db_path):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert c.get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200\n    accounts.set_active(db_path, DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"][\"email\"], False)\n    response = c.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code == 303 and response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\"\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"path\", [\"/admin/pages\", \"/admin/users\"])\ndef test_editor_is_refused_from_admin_areas_and_admin_is_not(client_as, path):\n    assert client_as(\"editor\").get(path).status_code == 403\n    assert client_as(\"admin\").get(path).status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_editor_can_reach_the_dashboard(client_as):\n    assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200\n\n\ndef test_editor_does_not_see_admin_navigation(client_as):\n    html = client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin\").text\n    assert \"/admin/users\" not in html and \"/admin/pages\" not in html\n\n\ndef test_login_without_a_valid_csrf_token_is_rejected(client):\n    creds = {\"email\": ADMIN[\"email\"], \"password\": ADMIN[\"password\"]}\n    assert client.post(\"/login\", data=creds).status_code == 403\n    client.get(\"/login\")\n    assert client.post(\"/login\", data={**creds, \"csrf_token\": \"forged\"}\n                       ).status_code == 403\n    assert client.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_logout_without_a_valid_csrf_token_is_rejected(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"admin\")\n    assert c.post(\"/logout\").status_code == 403\n    assert c.post(\"/logout\", data={\"csrf_token\": \"forged\"}).status_code == 403\n    assert c.get(\"/admin\").status_code == 200  # still signed in\n\n\ndef test_a_token_from_another_session_is_rejected(client_as, db_path):\n    other = TestClient(create_app(db_path))\n    foreign = csrf_from(other.get(\"/login\"))\n    c = client_as(\"admin\")\n    assert c.post(\"/logout\", data={\"csrf_token\": foreign}).status_code == 403\n\n\ndef test_each_app_gets_its_own_database(tmp_path):\n    a, b = tmp_path / \"a.db\", tmp_path / \"b.db\"\n    accounts.seed_users(a, admin_password=\"pw-a\", editor_password=\"pw-e\")\n    ca, cb = TestClient(create_app(a)), TestClient(create_app(b))\n    assert sign_in(ca, ADMIN[\"email\"], \"pw-a\").status_code == 303\n    assert sign_in(cb, ADMIN[\"email\"], \"pw-a\").status_code == 401\n\n\ndef run_seed(tmp_path, **env):\n    import os\n    clean = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items()\n             if k not in (\"CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD\", \"CMS_EDITOR_PASSWORD\")}\n    return subprocess.run(\n        [sys.executable, str(ROOT / \"scripts\" / \"seed_demo.py\")],\n        env={**clean, \"CMS_DATABASE\": str(tmp_path / \"seeded.db\"), **env},\n        capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=ROOT)\n\n\ndef test_seed_refuses_without_passwords(tmp_path):\n    result = run_seed(tmp_path)\n    assert result.returncode != 0\n    assert not (tmp_path / \"seeded.db\").exists()\n\n\ndef test_seed_creates_admin_and_editor_from_environment(tmp_path):\n    result = run_seed(tmp_path, CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD=\"seed-admin-pw\",\n \"seeded.db\")\n    assert sign_in(TestClient(app), ADMIN[\"email\"], \"seed-admin-pw\").status_code == 303\n    assert sign_in(TestClient(app), DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"][\"email\"],\n                   \"seed-editor-pw\").status_code == 303\n    # Running it twice is harmless.\n    again = run_seed(tmp_path, CMS_ADMIN_PASSWORD=\"seed-admin-pw\",\n {"parentUuid":"7b1c84c1-c33f-41e5-8129-b2b809b47039","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfYJneRzvoUBCGVp66Tf1","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Ln2HhXZJsrqNzReNKUK98u","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"mkdir -p app/routes templates/admin && touch app/routes/__init__.py\ncat > app/db.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"SQLite access. No ORM (ADR-002): a connection per request, plain SQL.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id              INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    name            TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    email           TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,\n    password_hash   TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    role            TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n    session_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    created_at      TEXT    NOT NULL\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path | str) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    # Sync FastAPI dependencies and endpoints may run on different threads.\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(path), check_same_thread=False)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db(path: Path | str) -> None:\n    conn = connect(path)\n    try:\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\nEOF\ncat > app/accounts.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Users: argon2 hashing, authentication, and Deactivation.\n\nUsers are never deleted, only Deactivated (see CONTEXT.md).\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHashError, VerificationError\n\nfrom app import db\n\n_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n# Verified against when the email is unknown, so a miss costs as much as a hit.\n_DUMMY_HASH = _hasher.hash(\"not-a-real-password\")\n\n\ndef create_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, name: str, email: str,\n                password: str, role: str) -> None:\n    conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO users (name, email, password_hash, role, created_at) \"\n        \"VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (name, email.strip(), _hasher.hash(password), role,\n         datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()),\n    )\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef authenticate(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str,\n                 password: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    \"\"\"The active User with this email and password, else None (never says why).\"\"\"\n    user = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?\",\n                        (email.strip(),)).fetchone()\n    stored = user[\"password_hash\"] if user else _DUMMY_HASH\n    try:\n        _hasher.verify(stored, password)\n    except (VerificationError, InvalidHashError):\n        return None\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n        return None\n    return user\n\n\ndef get_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef end_sessions(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Invalidate every session cookie issued to this User.\"\"\"\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET session_version = session_version + 1 \"\n                 \"WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef set_active(path: Path | str, email: str, active: bool) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Deactivate or reactivate a User. Their content and authorship stay put.\"\"\"\n    conn = db.connect(path)\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET active = ? WHERE email = ?\",\n                     (int(active), email.strip()))\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef seed_users(path: Path | str, *, admin_password: str, editor_password: str,\n               admin_email: str = \"admin@example.test\",\n               editor_email: str = \"editor@example.test\") -> None:\n    \"\"\"Create the first Admin and Editor. Existing emails are left alone.\"\"\"\n    db.init_db(path)\n    conn = db.connect(path)\n    try:\n        for name, email, password, role in (\n            (\"Demo Admin\", admin_email, admin_password, \"admin\"),\n            (\"Demo Editor\", editor_email, editor_password, \"editor\"),\n        ):\n            exists = conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE email = ?\",\n                                  (email,)).fetchone()\n            if not exists:\n                create_user(conn, name=name, email=email, password=password,\n                            role=role)\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\nEOF\ncat > app/auth.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Permissions and CSRF: the checks shared by every route.\n\n- `csrf_protect` runs on every state-changing request in the app.\n- `current_user` is what makes a console route require login, and refuses a\n  Deactivated User on every request.\n- `require_admin` is the role check for Admin-only areas. Refusal is a 403.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hmac\nimport secrets\nimport sqlite3\nfrom typing import Iterator\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\n\nfrom app import accounts, db\n\nSAFE_METHODS = {\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"}\n\n\nclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised when a request needs a signed-in User and has none.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    conn = db.connect(request.app.state.db_path)\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    \"\"\"This session's CSRF token, created on first use.\"\"\"\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def csrf_protect(request: Request) -> None:\n    if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:\n        return\n    sent = request.headers.get(\"x-csrf-token\")\n    if sent is None:\n        sent = (await request.form()).get(\"csrf_token\")\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if (not expected or not isinstance(sent, str)\n            or not hmac.compare_digest(sent, expected)):\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"Invalid or missing CSRF token.\")\n\n\ndef sign_in(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    # Fresh session and token on login, so nothing set before it carries over.\n    request.session.clear()\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user[\"id\"]\n    request.session[\"session_version\"] = user[\"session_version\"]\n    request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request,\n                 conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    user = accounts.get_user(conn, user_id) if user_id else None\n    if (user is None or not user[\"active\"]\n            or user[\"session_version\"] != request.session.get(\"session_version\")):\n        request.session.clear()\n        raise LoginRequired()\n    return user\n\n\ndef require_admin(user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(current_user)) -> sqlite3.Row:\n    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"This area is for Admins only.\")\n    return user\nEOF\ncat > app/web.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Shared Jinja environment for the console.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.auth import csrf_token\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\ntemplates.env.globals[\"csrf_token\"] = csrf_token\nEOF\ncat > app/routes/auth.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Login and logout.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import accounts, auth\nfrom app.web import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter()\n\nGENERIC_ERROR = \"Wrong email or password.\"\n\n\n@router.get(\"/login\")\ndef login_form(request: Request):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, \"admin/login.html\",\n                                      {\"title\": \"Sign in\", \"error\": None})\n\n\n@router.post(\"/login\")\ndef login(request: Request, email: str = Form(\"\"), password: str = Form(\"\"),\n          conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    user = accounts.authenticate(conn, email, password)\n    if user is None:\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"admin/login.html\",\n            {\"title\": \"Sign in\", \"error\": GENERIC_ERROR}, status_code=401)\n    auth.sign_in(request, user)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/logout\")\ndef logout(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user),\n           conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(auth.get_db)):\n    accounts.end_sessions(conn, user[\"id\"])\n    request.session.clear()\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\nEOF\ncat > app/routes/console.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"The console: every route here requires a signed-in User.\n\nThe router-level dependency is what guarantees that, so a new route can't\nforget it. Pages and Users are Admin-only placeholders until T06 and T07.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Request\n\nfrom app import auth\nfrom app.web import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.current_user)])\n\n\ndef page(request: Request, template: str, user: sqlite3.Row, title: str):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, template,\n                                      {\"title\": title, \"user\": user})\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef dashboard(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return page(request, \"admin/dashboard.html\", user, \"Dashboard\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"/pages\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_admin)])\ndef pages(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return page(request, \"admin/placeholder.html\", user, \"Pages\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"/users\", dependencies=[Depends(auth.require_admin)])\ndef users(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(auth.current_user)):\n    return page(request, \"admin/placeholder.html\", user, \"Users\")\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{path:path}\")\ndef not_found(path: str):\n    # Signed-out visitors were already redirected; this is a real 404.\n    from fastapi import HTTPException\n    raise HTTPException(404, \"Not found\")\nEOF\ncat > app/main.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nThe console lives under /admin (plus /login and /logout); the public site\nanswers at /. Add routes in their own modules under app/routes/ and include\nthem here. No ORM (ADR-002): a connection per request, plain SQL.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id              INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    name            TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    email           TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,\n    password_hash   TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    role            TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    active          INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n    session_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    created_at      TEXT    NOT NULL\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path | str) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    # Sync FastAPI dependencies and endpoints may run on different threads.\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(path), check_same_thread=False)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON\")\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db(path: Path | str) -> None:\n    conn = connect(path)\n    try:\n        conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\nEOF\ncat > app/accounts.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Users: argon2 hashing, authentication, and Deactivation.\n\nUsers are never deleted, only Deactivated (see CONTEXT.md).\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom argon2 import PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHashError, VerificationError\n\nfrom app import db\n\n_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n# Verified against when the email is unknown, so a miss costs as much as a hit.\n_DUMMY_HASH = _hasher.hash(\"not-a-real-password\")\n\n\ndef create_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, name: str, email: str,\n                password: str, role: str) -> None:\n    conn.execute(\n        \"INSERT INTO users (name, email, password_hash, role, created_at) \"\n        \"VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n        (name, email.strip(), _hasher.hash(password), role,\n         datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()),\n    )\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef authenticate(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str,\n                 password: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    \"\"\"The active User with this email and password, else None (never says why).\"\"\"\n    user = conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = ?\",\n                        (email.strip(),)).fetchone()\n    stored = user[\"password_hash\"] if user else _DUMMY_HASH\n    try:\n        _hasher.verify(stored, password)\n    except (VerificationError, InvalidHashError):\n        return None\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n        return None\n    return user\n\n\ndef get_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef end_sessions(conn: sqlite3.Connection, user_id: int) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Invalidate every session cookie issued to this User.\"\"\"\n    conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET session_version = session_version + 1 \"\n                 \"WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,))\n    conn.commit()\n\n\ndef set_active(path: Path | str, email: str, active: bool) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Deactivate or reactivate a User. Their content and authorship stay put.\"\"\"\n    conn = db.connect(path)\n    try:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE users SET active = ? WHERE email = ?\",\n                     (int(active), email.strip()))\n        conn.commit()\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef seed_users(path: Path | str, *, admin_password: str, editor_password: str,\n               admin_email: str = \"admin@example.test\",\n               editor_email: str = \"editor@example.test\") -> None:\n    \"\"\"Create the first Admin and Editor. Existing emails are left alone.\"\"\"\n    db.init_db(path)\n    conn = db.connect(path)\n    try:\n        for name, email, password, role in (\n            (\"Demo Admin\", admin_email, admin_password, \"admin\"),\n            (\"Demo Editor\", editor_email, editor_password, \"editor\"),\n        ):\n            exists = conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE email = ?\",\n                                  (email,)).fetchone()\n            if not exists:\n                create_user(conn, name=name, email=email, password=password,\n                            role=role)\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\nEOF\ncat > app/auth.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Permissions and CSRF: the checks shared by every route.\n\n- `csrf_protect` runs on every state-changing request in the app.\n- `current_user` is what makes a console route require login, and refuses a\n  Deactivated User on every request.\n- `require_admin` is the role check for Admin-only areas. app import accounts, db\n\nSAFE_METHODS = {\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"}\n\n\nclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised when a request needs a signed-in User and has none.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:\n    conn = db.connect(request.app.state.db_path)\n    try:\n        yield conn\n    finally:\n        conn.close()\n\n\ndef db","+","+_hasher = PasswordHasher()","+# Verified against when the email is unknown, so a miss costs as much as a hit.","+_DUMMY_HASH = _hasher.hash(\"not-a-real-password\")","+","+","+def create_user(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, name: str, email: str,","+                password: str, role: str) -> None:","+    conn.execute(","+        \"INSERT INTO users (name, email, password_hash, conn.commit()","+","+","+def authenticate(conn: sqlite3.Connection, email: str,","+                 password: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:","+    \"\"\"The active User with this email and password, Existing emails are left alone.\"\"\"","+    db.init_db(path)","+    conn = db.connect(path)","+    try:","+        for name, email, password, role in (","+            (\"Demo Admin\", admin_email, admin_password, \"admin\"),","+            (\"Demo Editor\", editor_email, editor_password, \"editor\"),","+        ):","+            exists = conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE email = ?\",","+                                  (email,)).fetchone()","+            if not exists:","+                create_user(conn, name=name, email=email, password=password,","+                            role=role)","+    finally:","+        conn.close()"]}],"created":true},{"filePath":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/auth.py","hunks":[{"oldStart":0,"oldLines":0,"newStart":1,"newLines":76,"lines":["+\"\"\"Permissions and CSRF: the checks shared by every route.","+","+- `csrf_protect` runs on every state-changing request in the app.","+- `current_user` is what makes a console route require login, and refuses a","+  Deactivated User on every request.","+- `require_admin` is the role check for Admin-only areas. Refusal is a 403.","+\"\"\"","+from __future__ import annotations","+","+import hmac","+import secrets","+import sqlite3","+from typing import Iterator","+","+from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request","+","+from app import accounts, db","+","+SAFE_METHODS = {\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"}","+","+","+class LoginRequired(Exception):","+    \"\"\"Raised when a request needs a signed-in User and has none.\"\"\"","+","+","+def get_db(request: Request) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:","+    conn = db.connect(request.app.state.db_path)","+    try:","+        yield conn","+    finally:","+        conn.close()","+","+","+def csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:","+    \"\"\"This session's CSRF token, created on first use.\"\"\"","+    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")","+    if not token:","+        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)","+    return token","+","+","+async def csrf_protect(request: Request) -> None:","+    if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:","+        return","+    sent = request.headers.get(\"x-csrf-token\")","+    if sent is None:","+        sent = (await request.form()).get(\"csrf_token\")","+    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\")","+    if (not expected or not isinstance(sent, str)","+            or not hmac.compare_digest(sent, expected)):","+        raise HTTPException(403, \"Invalid or missing CSRF token.\")","+","+","+def sign_in(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row) -> None:","+    # Fresh session and token on login, so nothing set before it carries over.","+    request.session.clear()","+    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user[\"id\"]","+    request.session[\"session_version\"] = user[\"session_version\"]","+    request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)","+","+","+def current_user(request: Request,","+                 conn: sqlite3.Connection = Depends(get_db)) -> sqlite3.Row:","+    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")","+    user = accounts.get_user(conn, user_id) if user_id else None","+    if (user is None or not user[\"active\"]","+            or user[\"session_version\"] != request.session.get(\"session_version\")):","+        request.session.clear()","+        raise LoginRequired()","+    return user","+","+","+def require_admin(user: sqlite3.Row = Depends(current_user)) -> sqlite3.Row:","+    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":","+        raise HTTPException(403, \"This area is for Admins only.\")","+    return user"]}],"created":true},{"filePath":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/cli.py","hunks":[{"oldStart":28,"oldLines":7,"newStart":28,"newLines":8,"lines":["     if args.command == \"serve\":","         import uvicorn"," ","-        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)","+        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:create_app\", factory=True, port=args.port,","+                    reload=args.reload)","         return 0"," ","     if args.command == \"publish\":"]}]},{"filePath":"/home/gianlucabocanegra/iphs400-mp2-cms/app/db.py","hunks":[{"oldStart":0,"oldLines":0,"newStart":1,"newLines":35,"lines":["+\"\"\"SQLite access.
